### [84] [Bootstrapping Niche Multilingual Code Translation via Reinforcement Learning with Execution-Based Verifiable Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13854)
*Kouki Yuki,Jie Zeng,Kyoko Ogawa,Ryunosuke Ikeda,Yohei Kobashi,Takeshi Kojima,Ikuya Yamada,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于执行监督的偏好强化学习方法，用于解决多语言到多语言（many-to-many）的代码翻译问题。通过扩展可验证的种子Python程序生成多语言执行验证池，利用偏好数据训练奖励模型，最终用GRPO优化25种语言之间的600个方向对翻译。引入HumanEval-X++基准，在4B模型上平均提升13%，中低资源语言提升21%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经代码翻译主要聚焦少数主流语言（如C++、Java、Python），缺乏对广泛多语言、多向翻译场景的支持。在平行监督数据稀疏的many-to-many设置下，现有方法容易生成语法上看似合理但无法执行的翻译结果，因此需要一种不依赖大量平行数据的训练方法。

Method: 1) 将可验证的种子Python程序扩展为多语言执行验证代码池；2) 基于该代码池，基础LLM生成跨语言翻译候选并通过执行结果标注偏好；3) 用偏好数据训练奖励模型以评估跨语言翻译质量；4) 使用GRPO算法覆盖25×24=600个语言方向对进行优化；5) 构建HumanEval-X++执行基准以评估广泛多语言翻译能力。

Result: 使用Qwen-3.5 4B和9B模型进行验证，在HumanEval-X++及现有基准上均获得一致提升。4B模型在HumanEval-X++上所有语言平均提升13%，中端（mid-tier）语言上增益达21%。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了可靠的数据生成、训练与基准评测流程，证明了基于执行监督的偏好强化学习可有效提升稀疏监督场景下的多语言到多语言代码翻译质量，为该方向进一步扩展奠定了基础。

Abstract: Code translation must preserve executable behavior across many programming languages, yet neural code translation has largely focused on a few popular languages such as C++, Java, and Python. This leaves a niche, many-to-many setting where parallel supervision is sparse, producing plausible but non-executable translations. We address this setting with preference-based reinforcement learning driven by execution-based supervision. Our pipeline firstly expands verifiable seed Python programs into a multilingual pool of execution-validated codes. Using the pool, a base LLM generates translation candidates across language pairs, which we label by their execution outcomes. The resulting preferences are used to train a reward model that scores cross-language translation quality. Finally, we optimize our base LLMs with GRPO over 600 directed language pairs (25 x 24) using the reward model as a signal. To evaluate the niche translation capability, we introduce HumanEval-X++, an execution-based benchmark that extends HumanEval-X to a broad many-to-many language space. We evaluate our approach using Qwen-3.5 4B and 9B models. On HumanEval-X++ and existing benchmarks, it yields consistent gains over the untrained baselines. In particular, the 4B model achieves an average improvement of 13% across all languages on HumanEval-X++, with a gain of 21% on mid-tier languages. Our study establishes a reliable approach of data generation, training, and benchmarking, paving the way toward further bootstrapping the quality of many-to-many translation for programming languages.

</details>


### [85] [Scaling Creative Writing Beyond Story-Centric Data with Attribute-Guided Genre Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13947)
*Hwan Chang,Yongil Kim,Heuiyeen Yeen,Yireun Kim,Jinsik Lee,Hwanhee Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种基于属性引导的体裁扩展框架（attribute-guided genre expansion），将主题广度与体裁形式控制分离，利用人类标注的故事提示作为创意种子，配合人工策划的体裁属性来约束结构、风格和格式规范，构建了覆盖13种创意体裁、5万样本的Multi-Genre Collection语料库。实验表明在该数据上微调的模型在分布外写作基准和保留体裁诊断上均优于基座模型、写作专用基线和现有写作语料训练的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高质量创意写作数据以故事类（story-centric）为主，导致大语言模型难以遵循多样化创意体裁的结构性和功能性惯例。作者希望突破故事生成的局限，扩展创意写作数据到更多体裁。

Method: 提出属性引导的体裁扩展框架，核心思想是将主题广度（thematic breadth）与体裁形式控制（genre-form control）解耦。具体做法：(1) 使用人类撰写的故事提示作为多样化创意种子；(2) 人工策划体裁属性以强制不同的结构、风格和格式约定；(3) 将两者结合提示强LLM生成符合体裁的问答对；(4) 通过质量过滤筛选。最终构建Multi-Genre Collection，含50K样本，覆盖13种创意体裁。

Result: 在分布外写作基准和保留体裁诊断实验中，使用该数据微调的模型持续超越基座模型、写作专用基线和现有写作语料训练的模型。体裁数量消融实验表明，受控的体裁扩展而非仅故事中心缩放，是提升创意写作能力的关键驱动因素。

Conclusion: 受控的体裁扩展是提升LLM创意写作鲁棒性的核心驱动力，仅靠扩大故事类数据规模不足以带来同样的能力提升。该框架为创意写作数据规模化提供了可泛化的范式。

Abstract: High-quality creative writing data for large language models (LLMs) remains dominated by story-centric data, limiting models' ability to follow the structural and functional conventions of diverse creative formats. We propose an attribute-guided genre expansion framework for scaling creative writing data beyond story generation. By separating thematic breadth from genre-form control, our framework leverages human-authored story prompts as diverse creative seeds, while utilizing manually curated genre attributes to enforce distinct structural, stylistic, and formatting conventions. We combine these to prompt strong LLMs for genre-faithful query-response pairs, which are then quality-filtered. Applying this framework, we construct the Multi-Genre Collection, a 50K-example corpus spanning 13 creative genres, including story, rap, lyrics, scripts, game design, character design, and other creative formats. Experiments across out-of-distribution writing benchmarks and held-out genre diagnostics demonstrate that models fine-tuned on our data consistently surpass not only base models and writing-specialized baselines, but also models trained on existing writing corpora. Genre-count ablations further indicate that controlled genre expansion, rather than story-centric scaling alone, is a key driver of robust creative writing capability.

</details>


### [86] [Repair, Not Improvement: Decomposing Constrained Decoding in Tool-Call Abstention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13959)
*Janghoon Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文测量了受约束生成中被先前工作明确排除的场景——工具放弃（tool abstention）。在字节相同的提示下，文法承担两个职能：决定生成何时停止以及允许发射哪些token。作者在0.6B到4B的开源模型上用匹配的英文和韩文条目进行对照，发现与无约束解码器相比，先前工作的对比在六个单元格中有四个在放弃任务上为负（最差-29.5分），没有一个为正且置信区间排除零。总体效果是正负号相反的加和，文法主要恢复答案的形式而非内容正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前关于受限生成（constrained generation）的研究明确将函数调用（function calling）排除在外，并警告不要将格式约束的结论外推到涉及正确性要求的约束上。工具放弃是最极端的情况：枚举类型只是缩小答案集合而非改变措辞，而拒绝调用任何工具是首先被丢弃的选项。作者旨在测量这个被排除的案例，检验文法约束在工具放弃场景中的实际贡献。

Method: 实验设计采用三个条件，每个条件使用字节相同的提示，以此分离文法的两个职能：一是决定生成停止的位置（停止token），二是决定可发射的token集合（枚举）。评估对象为0.6B到4B的开源权重模型，在匹配的英文和韩文条目上进行测试，使得语言对比在条目内进行。预注册了测量指标（预注册的放弃度量）和两个语言假设。

Result: 在放弃任务上，先前工作的对比在四个单元格中为负且置信区间排除零（最差-29.5个百分点），没有任何单元格为正且置信区间排除零。总体效果是正负号相消：在最小模型的韩文条件下，停止token代价为-20.0，枚举返回+19.5，合计-0.5。恢复的内容主要是形式而非实质：698个被修复的放弃中，545个没有可读答案，0个是被评分者拒绝的判断。在需要工具的项目上，效果全为正向；预注册的放弃度量使干预效果看起来更差，而汇合数字对干预更有利反而让转换变得更糟。两个预注册的语言假设均失败。

Conclusion: 在工具放弃这一最极端的正确性约束场景中，文法约束对开放权重模型的作用与先前关于格式约束的结论不同：放弃任务上约束可能是负面的（总体为负或接近零），且其恢复的主要是答案的形式而非正确性。即使在不改变提示措辞的情况下，仅由停止token与枚举组合带来的效果也高度不对称。本文的测量同时证明：预注册的度量选择会实质性地改变结论方向，且语言间效果不具一致性。

Abstract: Function calling is what the recent accounting of constrained generation explicitly sets aside: it finds the decoder's contribution small for format constraints, then warns in its Section 7 against extrapolating where a constraint encodes a correctness requirement, and names function calling as one. Tool abstention is that case at its sharpest: an enum leaves the wording of an answer alone and narrows the set of answers there are, and declining to call anything is the first it drops. We measure the excluded case. Three conditions over one byte-identical prompt separate a grammar's two jobs: it fixes where generation stops as well as which tokens may be emitted. We evaluate open-weight models from 0.6B to 4B on matched English and Korean items, so the language comparison is made within item. Against an unconstrained decoder, prior work's contrast is negative on abstention in four of six cells with intervals excluding zero, worst -29.5 points, and positive with an interval excluding zero in none. The total is a sum with opposite signs: on the smallest model in Korean the stop token costs -20.0, the enum returns +19.5, and the two leave -0.5. What it recovers is form: of 698 abstentions repaired, 545 had no readable answer and 0 were judgements the scorer refused. On tool-needed items it is positive throughout; abstention leads because it is the preregistered measure, and the pooled number being kinder to the intervention makes moving to it worse rather than better. Both preregistered language claims fail.

</details>


### [87] [Batch-wise Adaptive Pruning: Periodic Neuron Activation-Aware Weight Pruning for Language Reasoning Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14003)
*Yongmin Kim,Shota Takashiro,Yusuke Iwasawa,Takeshi Kojima,Yutaka Matsuo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种专为LRM批处理推理设计的免训练自适应剪枝方法，解决现有阈值选择方法在批处理场景下因激活分布偏移导致稀疏率漂移、推理精度崩溃的问题，通过周期性top-k选择替代阈值选择并结合激活记忆机制，在DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B上以50%目标稀疏度达到39.7个百分点的平均精度提升和1.40倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型（LRMs）通过扩展思维链获得强性能但推理成本高昂。批处理推理是高吞吐量的必要条件，然而现有免训练自适应剪枝方法在批处理场景下严重失效：由于批次共享单一剪枝掩码，这些方法聚合各样本激活后做阈值选择，而离线校准的阈值不再匹配聚合后的分布，导致实际稀疏率漂移、推理精度崩溃。因此需要专门为LRM批处理推理设计的自适应剪枝方法。

Method: 提出两个核心组件：(1) 用周期性top-k选择替代阈值选择，在聚合重要性分数上操作，不受激活分布偏移影响，且每个更新周期只执行一次选择而非每个token执行，保持加速效果；(2) 基于重要神经元在长时间推理生成中周期性重新激活的观察，引入激活记忆机制，跨更新阶段累积重要性分数，确保周期性复现的神经元被保留。

Result: 在多种推理基准上，该方法在DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B上以批大小4、50%目标稀疏度下，平均精度比之前最先进的自适应剪枝方法高出39.7个百分点，并在50%实际稀疏度下达到相对密集推理1.40倍的速度提升。

Conclusion: 通过针对批处理推理场景重新设计免训练自适应剪枝的选择机制，成功解决了聚合激活导致的分布偏移问题，在保持加速收益的同时显著恢复了推理精度，为LRM在生产环境中的高效批处理推理提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) achieve strong performance on complex tasks through extended chain-of-thought generation, but incur substantial computational costs during inference. In production settings, batched inference is essential for high throughput, yet the existing training-free adaptive pruning methods we evaluate severely degrade in this regime. Because a batch must share a single pruning mask, these methods aggregate activations across samples and then apply threshold-based selection; the threshold, calibrated offline on unaggregated activations, no longer matches the aggregated distribution, so the realized sparsity ratio drifts and accuracy on reasoning tasks collapses under batched inference.
  In this work, we propose a training-free adaptive pruning method designed specifically for batched inference in LRMs, built on two components. First, we replace threshold-based selection with periodic top-k selection over the aggregated importance scores, which is unaffected by the shift that aggregation induces in the activation distribution, and which runs selection once per update period rather than at every token, preserving the speedup. Second, based on the observation that important neurons re-fire periodically during long reasoning generation, we introduce an activation memory that accumulates importance across update phases so that recurring neurons are retained.
  Experiments on diverse reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that our method outperforms the previous state-of-the-art adaptive pruning method by 39.7 percentage points in average accuracy at batch size 4 with 50% target sparsity on DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B, and reaches 1.40x speedup over dense inference at 50% actual sparsity.

</details>


### [88] [S2Dialog: Multimodal Dialogue Retrieval with Semantic and Acoustic-Style Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14029)
*Xueqi Wang,Zhigang Wang,Runqing Zhang,Zhenqi Jia,Junfeng Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: S2Dialog是一个统一框架，用于从多模态对话库中进行对话级别的语义—风格检索，通过对话级文本检索器和对话级声学检索器分别编码文本与声学模态，并引入对话级文本—声学对比学习来对齐相似对话，在DailyTalk数据集上取得了显著的检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索方法多局限于话语级或单模态匹配，难以捕捉整个对话的全局语义连贯性和风格一致性。然而多模态对话检索对情绪识别、口语对话系统、对话语音合成等任务至关重要，因此需要一种能够实现对话级多模态语义与风格检索的方法。

Method: 提出S2Dialog统一框架，包含两个核心组件：对话级文本检索器（Dialogue-level Textual Retriever）和对话级声学检索器（Dialogue-level Acoustic Retriever），分别将对话的文本和声学模态编码为对话级表示；此外引入对话级文本—声学对比学习（Dialogue-level Textual-Acoustic Contrastive Learning），在语义和风格上对齐相似对话并区分不相关对话。

Result: 在多模态对话数据集DailyTalk上的大量实验表明，S2Dialog取得了出色的检索性能。

Conclusion: S2Dialog通过对话级双模态检索器与跨模态对比学习的结合，有效提升了多模态对话库中的对话级检索能力，弥补了现有方法在全局语义与风格一致性捕获上的不足。

Abstract: Multimodal dialogue retrieval aims to retrieve dialogues from multimodal dialogue banks that are similar to a target dialogue in terms of both textual semantics and acoustic conversational styles. Such dialogue-level retrieval is crucial for many dialogue-related tasks, including Emotion Recognition in Conversation, Spoken Dialogue Systems, and Conversational Speech Synthesis, where external dialogue examples can provide valuable semantic and stylistic references. However, existing retrieval methods are still largely limited to utterance-level or unimodal matching, and often fail to capture the global semantic coherence and stylistic consistency of an entire dialogue. To address this gap, we propose S2Dialog, a unified framework for dialogue-level semantic-style retrieval from multimodal dialogue banks. Specifically, S2Dialog consists of a Dialogue-level Textual Retriever and a Dialogue-level Acoustic Retriever, which encode the textual and acoustic modalities of a dialogue into dialogue-level representations, respectively. To further enhance multimodal retrieval, we introduce Dialogue-level Textual-Acoustic Contrastive Learning, which aligns semantically and stylistically similar dialogues while distinguishing unrelated ones. Extensive experiments on the multimodal dialogue dataset DailyTalk demonstrate that S2Dialog achieves outstanding retrieval performance.

</details>


### [89] [HERMES: a multi-agent framework for structured knowledge extraction from ultra-long documents in geoscience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14055)
*Ziqi Song,Zongyuan Xiang,James G. Ogg,Bruce S. Lieberman,Gabi Ogg,Natalia López Carranza,Wen Du,Yufei Ye,Shuan Li,Zhong Peng,Shaoqi Yu,Juye Wei,Ying Zhou,Jieping Ye,Jiang Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍HERMES，一个可扩展的多智能体框架，用于从超长科学文献中提取结构化数据。应用于55卷《无脊椎古生物学论丛》，生成了32,277个化石分类实体和451,878个属性的结构化数据库，实体F1约0.90、属性F1约0.91，效率较人工基线提升约六倍，且无需额外训练即可迁移至古地磁学和地球化学领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球科学领域的权威科学知识大多封存于传统专著和历史文献中，非结构化文本和复杂版面严重阻碍了计算化访问。需要一种可扩展的方法将这些历史文献转化为符合FAIR原则的结构化数据，以支撑数据密集型学科和大规模知识整合。

Method: HERMES采用协调式大语言模型作为核心，构建多智能体框架，将领域约束、验证规则和证据追踪整合到统一的文档级提取流程中，同时解析文本、表格、图形和说明文字。系统应用于55卷《无脊椎古生物学论丛》进行全量提取验证，并在古地磁学和地球化学领域进行零训练迁移评估。

Result: 系统产出了包含32,277个化石分类实体和451,878个属性的结构化数据库（已发布于https://treatise.geolex.org）。跨化石类群提取性能稳定，实体平均F1约0.90，属性平均F1约0.91；每卷提取效率相较全人工基线提升约六倍。在古地磁学和地球化学上的迁移实验未额外训练即表现良好，验证了跨领域泛化能力。

Conclusion: HERMES提供了一条将历史科学文献转化为FAIR导向结构化数据的实用路径，为数据密集型学科构建可持续的基础设施和大规模知识集成体系奠定了基础。

Abstract: Authoritative scientific knowledge in geoscience remains largely trapped in legacy monographs and historical literature, where unstructured text and complex layouts hinder computational access. We introduce HERMES, a scalable multi-agent framework that extracts structured data from ultra-long scientific documents. Using a coordinating large language model, HERMES integrates domain constraints, validation rules and evidence tracing within a unified document-level extraction process that incorporates parsed text, tables, figures and captions. Applied to the 55-volume Treatise on Invertebrate Paleontology, the system produced a structured database of 32,277 fossil taxonomic entities and 451,878 attributes, released online at https://treatise.geolex.org. Extraction performance remained stable across fossil groups (average F1 scores of approximately 0.90 for entities and 0.91 for attributes), improving per-volume efficiency approximately sixfold relative to the tested fully manual baseline. Evaluation in palaeomagnetism and geochemistry, conducted without additional model training, demonstrated transfer across distinct geoscience domains. This work provides a practical pathway to transform historical scientific literature into FAIR-oriented structured data, offering a sustainable infrastructure for data-intensive disciplines and large-scale knowledge integration.

</details>


### [90] [The conditional superiority of fast silicon sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14079)
*Nickolas Hock Yuen Lam,Ji Xuan Voo,Xiangyu Ma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文比较了"快速"与"慢速"两种硅基采样（silicon sampling）模式在算法保真度上的表现，基于新加坡全国代表性调查样本。研究发现硅基采样仍是早期开发中的方法，需谨慎使用；在给定局限性下，快速模式在计算效率和算法保真度上均优于慢速模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硅基采样（即用大语言模型模拟人类调查受访者）在人口估计方面有时能产生出人意料的好结果，但尚不清楚快速执行是否会削弱这种保真度。作者希望系统评估快速与慢速两种硅基采样模式之间的算法保真度差异。

Method: 作者在新加坡全国代表性调查受访者样本中，将硅基采样分为"快速"和"慢速"两种模式，比较其算法保真度。通过评估对人口均值的估计准确性、意见方差的反映程度以及对潜在地缘上下文空间的还原能力来衡量两种模式的优劣。

Result: 硅基采样虽能产生中等保真度的人口均值估计，但仍低估意见方差并扭曲人类意见背后的潜在地缘上下文空间。在此局限条件下，快速模式在计算资源和运行时间上显著更高效，且在算法保真度上对照慢速模式呈单调优胜。

Conclusion: 硅基采样仍处于早期发展阶段，使用须极度谨慎。但在给定其固有局限的前提下，快速硅基采样模式是比传统慢速模式更优的选择，兼具效率和保真度优势。

Abstract: Silicon sampling can produce surprisingly good population estimates at times. Does doing it fast attenuate such fidelity? In this study, we extend and assess ongoing work in silicon sampling by comparing the algorithmic fidelity of "fast" and "slow" modes of silicon sampling among a nationally representative sample of Singaporean survey respondents. We find that silicon sampling with contemporary frontier models remains a method in early development to be used only with great caution. While silicon samples are able to produce moderately faithful estimates of population means, they continue to understate opinion variance and distort the latent contextual space behind human opinions. Conditional on such limitations, we find "fast" modes of silicon sampling to be relatively superior to traditional "slow" modes of silicon sampling. Fast silicon sampling is significantly more efficient in compute resources and run-time while being monotonically superior to slower modes of sampling in algorithmic fidelity.

</details>


### [91] [Leading-Silence Augmentation and Multi-Stage Synthetic Supervision for the Second MLC-SLM Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14150)
*Kexin Shi,Renhe Sun,Yuge Huang,Ximeng Wang,Jiayi Zhou,Jian Liu,Malu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文报告了在第二届多语言对话语音语言模型（MLC-SLM）挑战赛中的技术方案与结果。针对说话人日志与识别任务（Task 1），作者对VibeVoice-ASR-7B模型采用随机前导静音裁剪、时间戳一致性校正及EMA训练策略，将tcpMER从18.30%降至16.73%。针对对话语音理解任务（Task 2），通过多模态候选生成、静音音频过滤和分布匹配增强构建合成问答对，并微调Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B-Instruct进行标签化直接回答，将准确率从83.0%提升至86.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两届MLC-SLM挑战赛在完整未分段的多语言对话上设定任务，且评估时既不提供预言边界也不提供说话人标签，Task 2甚至不提供问答训练集，这使得传统依赖句子切分和标注数据的解决方案难以直接应用，需要探索新的训练策略与数据构造方法。

Method: Task 1：对VibeVoice-ASR-7B进行微调，采用随机前导静音裁剪（random leading-silence cropping）、一致的时间戳校正（consistent timestamp correction）和指数移动平均（EMA）训练策略。Task 2：通过多模态候选生成、静音音频过滤和分布匹配增强（distribution-matched augmentation）构造合成问答对，并微调Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B-Instruct，采用标注化直接回答（tagged direct answering）方式。

Result: Task 1评估集上，静音裁剪将tcpMER从18.30%降至17.27%，EMA进一步降至16.73%。Task 2评估集上，联合应用分布匹配增强和标签化直接回答将准确率从83.0%提升至86.0%。

Conclusion: 多种针对无分段对话场景的微调与数据增强策略均有效提升了语音日志识别和对话理解的性能，验证了在不依赖Oracle边界和问答监督数据条件下，通过精巧的训练技巧与合成数据构造可获得显著的性能增益。

Abstract: The second Multilingual Conversational Speech Language Model (MLC-SLM) Challenge evaluates two tasks over complete, unsegmented multilingual conversations: speaker diarization and recognition (Task 1) and conversational speech understanding (Task 2). Neither task provides oracle utterance boundaries or speaker labels at evaluation, and Task 2 provides no question-answer training set. For Task 1, we fine-tune VibeVoice-ASR-7B with random leading-silence cropping, consistent timestamp correction, and an exponential moving average (EMA) training strategy. For Task 2, we construct synthetic question-answer pairs through multimodal candidate generation, silent-audio filtering, and distribution-matched augmentation, and fine-tune Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B-Instruct for tagged direct answering. On the Task 1 evaluation set, cropping reduces tcpMER from 18.30% to 17.27%, and EMA further reduces it to 16.73%. On the Task 2 evaluation set, jointly applying distribution-matched augmentation and tagged direct answering raises accuracy from 83.0% to 86.0%.

</details>


### [92] [How Much Do Legal RAG Systems Still Hallucinate?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14210)
*Souvick Das,Sallam Abualhaija,Domenico Bianculli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Hallucination is a major challenge for retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems in the legal domain, where ungrounded answers can lead to serious consequences. To better understand this problem, we conduct a fine-grained analysis of hallucination behavior in eight legal RAG systems across two legal corpora, the GDPR (in English) and a national civil law (in French). Using claim-level and answer-level evaluation, we report on hallucination density and severity, analyze performance across question categories and user personas, and validate our findings on an independent set of 142 legal-expert-authored questions. Our results show that hallucinations remain pervasive, ranging from less than 10% of responses for the best-performing systems to nearly half in the worst case. We further find that false-premise questions, containing incorrect assumptions that must be rejected, produce high hallucination rates on the manually-drafted questions.

</details>


### [93] [The More Popular, The Harder to Forget: Adaptive Popularity for LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14229)
*Anna Borisiuk,Andrey Savchenko,Alexander Panchenko,Elena Tutubalina*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AdaPop是一种自适应流行度LLM遗忘方法，基于事实的训练数据流行度调整遗忘梯度压力，并通过双上升控制器自动平衡遗忘与保留，在三个模型族和两个基准上使泄漏量较对比方法减少约5倍（释义查询）和1.6倍（对抗改写）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM遗忘方法对所有训练事实施加统一的梯度压力，忽视了高频（流行）事实与低频（稀有）事实在预训练中的记忆深度和遗忘难度差异，导致遗忘效果不佳或保留性能受损。

Method: AdaPop将局部token置信度与基于外部代理（如Wikidata sitelinks、LLM-as-Judge）推导的逐事实流行度指数结合，并采用双上升控制器每轮自动调整保留惩罚项，实现遗忘-保留平衡的自动化。

Result: 在三个模型族和两个基准上，AdaPop在释义查询下泄露的遗忘内容约为对比方法的1/5，在对抗性改写下约为1/1.6。内部指标显示遗忘集隐藏状态较其他方法更远离未遗忘模型状态，而保留集表示保持接近。

Conclusion: 通过按流行度自适应地差异化施加遗忘压力，AdaPop显著提升了遗忘彻底性，同时有效维持了保留知识的完整性，验证了考虑数据频率差异在LLM遗忘中的必要性。

Abstract: Popular facts are memorised more deeply during pretraining and resist removal longer than rare ones, yet existing LLM unlearning methods apply uniform gradient pressure regardless of training-data frequency. We propose the AdaPop (Adaptive Popularity) method, which combines local token confidence with a per-fact popularity-dependent exponent derived from an external proxy (e.g., Wikidata sitelinks, LLM-as-Judge), and automates the forget-retain balance via a dual-ascent controller that adjusts the retain penalty each epoch. Across three model families and two benchmarks, AdaPop leaks ~5x less forgotten content than competing methods under paraphrased queries and ~1.6x less under adversarial reformulations. We support our analysis with internal metrics: under our method, forget-set hidden states move further from the pre-unlearning model's states than under other methods, while retain-set representations remain close.

</details>


### [94] [SimpleOPD: Simple Tokenizer-Agnostic On-Policy Distillation for Long-Context Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14277)
*Haonan He,Haodi Lei,Yun Luo,Haoran Zhang,Shunkai Zhang,Yizhuo Li,Shengji Tang,Zhilin Wang,Runzhe Zhan,Lei Bai,Ganqu Cui,Fangchen Yu,Yafu Li,Peng Ye,Ning Ding,Yu Cheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了从长上下文推理教师模型（SU-01）向短上下文学生模型进行同策略蒸馏（OPD）以迁移推理能力的问题。为解决分词器不匹配、师生分布不匹配、响应长度爆炸和训练不稳定等挑战，作者在共享文本空间进行OPD，仅对齐师生分词器下占据相同文本跨度的tokens，并引入学生参考KL损失及对终止特殊token的优势掩码。实验覆盖Qwen3、Qwen3.5、Intern-S2、GLM-4.7、Gemma-4等多个同族和异族模型，在数学推理特别是自然语言数学证明上取得一致提升；其中Intern-S2-Preview在ProofBench上提升21.2分至55.2，超越Gemini-2.5-Pro，并在HLE和HiPhO等科学基准上亦有提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同策略蒸馏（OPD）是将强教师模型的推理能力迁移到学生模型的有效途径，但将其应用于长上下文推理教师与短上下文学生的组合时，面临分词器不匹配、师生分布不匹配、响应长度爆炸和训练不稳定等实际挑战，需要针对这一特定设置提出有效的解决方案。

Method: 1）在共享文本空间中进行OPD，仅对齐学生和教师分词器下占据相同文本跨度的tokens，以处理分词器差异；2）引入学生参考KL损失，约束学生不过度偏离其初始策略，缓解师生分布不匹配；3）对</think>、<|im_end|>等特殊终止token的优势进行掩码，抑制响应长度爆炸并促进生成长度稳定增长。

Result: 在Qwen3、Qwen3.5、Intern-S2、GLM-4.7、Gemma-4等多个同族和异族学生模型上取得一致的数学推理能力提升，尤其体现在自然语言数学证明任务上。其中Intern-S2-Preview在ProofBench上提升21.2分达到55.2，超越Gemini-2.5-Pro；同时在HLE和HiPhO等科学基准上也有提升，表明OPD迁移的推理能力可泛化到数学训练领域之外。

Conclusion: 所提出的OPD方法（共享文本空间token对齐、学生参考KL损失、终止token优势掩码）能够有效解决长上下文教师向短上下文学生蒸馏时的关键技术挑战，实现推理能力的成功迁移，且迁移能力具有超越数学领域的泛化性。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) offers a promising way to transfer reasoning capabilities from stronger teacher models, but applying it to long-context reasoning teachers and short-context students introduces practical challenges, including tokenizer mismatch, teacher-student distribution mismatch, response length explosion, and training instability. In this work, we study this setting by transferring proof-reasoning capabilities from the long-context reasoning model SU-01 to short-context student models. To handle tokenizer differences, we perform OPD in a shared text space and align only tokens that occupy identical text spans under the student and teacher tokenizers. To mitigate the problem of excessive generation length and frequent truncation, we introduce a student reference KL loss and mask the advantages of special termination tokens such as </think> and <|im_end|>. This strategy constrains the student from drifting excessively from its initial policy, thereby mitigating the teacher-student distribution mismatch problem and fostering steady length growth. Experiments on both same-family and different-family student models, including Qwen3, Qwen3.5, Intern-S2, GLM-4.7, Gemma-4, show consistent gains in mathematical reasoning, especially natural-language math proving. Notably, Intern-S2-Preview improves by 21.2 points on ProofBench, reaching 55.2 and surpassing Gemini-2.5-Pro. It also improves on science benchmarks such as HLE and HiPhO, suggesting that OPD transfers reasoning capabilities that generalize beyond the mathematical training domain.

</details>


### [95] [Envs-FORGE: Frontier-Optimized Reward-Grounded Environment Synthesis for Agent RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14312)
*Xiaojun Wu,Cehao Yang,Honghao Liu,Xueyuan Lin,Zhichao Shi,Hao Zhou,Xuhui Jiang,Chengjin Xu,Jia Li,Jian Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Envs-FORGE，一种将验证器奖励转化为逐种子环境合成动作的提示策略，通过估计种子通过率、评估六个投影方向动作并求解逐种子混合整数线性规划（MILP）来选择最优动作，同步重写指令、固定装置、参考解、测试和Docker环境；仅金标准验证的捆绑包进入RL训练。在Qwen 3.5 35B上，tb-core Pass@1提升9.2个百分点（40.0%→49.2%），tb-2.0提升6.4个百分点（23.0%→29.4%），SWE-bench Verified达77.1%，超越最强固定配方基线2.4和2.1个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RL训练环境合成方法（如few-shot、Self-Instruct、Evol-Instruct）对所有种子应用相同的提示策略，忽略了不同种子对难度变化的不同需求，无法为当前策略自适应地提供更合适难度的任务。

Method: Envs-FORGE将RL中验证器奖励转化为逐种子的环境合成决策：估计每个种子的通过率，定义六个围绕目标学习前沿的投影方向动作，通过逐种子MILP选择最优动作，并驱动指令、fixtures、oracle解、测试和Docker环境的同步重写；仅将金标准验证通过的捆绑包送入RL训练。MILP形式还支持可选的软技能覆盖以进行组合规划。

Result: 在Qwen 3.5 35B上，Envs-FORGE在tb-core上将Pass@1从40.0%提升至49.2%（+9.2点），在tb-2.0上从23.0%提升至29.4%（+6.4点），分别超过最强固定配方基线2.4和2.1点；SWE-bench Verified达到77.1%（Base为73.4%）；在4B-35B多规模模型上tb-core提升6.8-9.2点。所有方法均导出100个验证环境，使用2.27M-2.88M合成token，保证同下游训练集规模和同操作规模对比。

Conclusion: 逐种子自适应环境合成策略显著优于固定配方方法，通过将强化学习验证器信号反哺到训练环境生成环节，实现了任务难度与当前策略能力更精准的匹配，且在不同模型规模上均展现稳定收益。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for terminal agents needs executable training environments with reliable rewards and useful difficulty. Fixed recipes such as few-shot, Self-Instruct, and Evol-Instruct apply the same prompting policy to every seed, even when the current policy would benefit from a harder, easier, or simply different task. We present Envs-FORGE, a prompting policy that converts verifier rewards into per-seed environment-synthesis actions. Envs-FORGE estimates seed pass rates, scores six projection--direction actions around a target learning frontier, and solves a per-seed mixed-integer linear program (MILP) to choose the action that conditions generation. The selected action drives synchronized rewriting of the instruction, fixtures, oracle solution, tests, and Docker environment; only gold-verified bundles enter RL training. The indexed MILP form also supports optional soft skill coverage for portfolio planning. On Qwen 3.5 35B, Envs-FORGE improves Pass@1 over Base by 9.2 percentage points on tb-core (40.0% to 49.2%) and 6.4 points on tb-2.0 (23.0% to 29.4%), exceeding the strongest fixed-recipe baseline by 2.4 and 2.1 points. It reaches 77.1% on SWE-bench Verified versus 73.4% for Base, and improves tb-core by 6.8--9.2 points across the evaluated 4B--35B models. All synthesis methods export 100 verified environments and use 2.27M--2.88M synthesis tokens, placing the comparison at the same downstream training-set size and the same operational scale. The source code is available at https://github.com/DataArcTech/DataArc-SynData-Toolkit/.

</details>


### [96] [Local and Global Regimes of Geometric Complexity in Language Model Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14361)
*Arwa Osman,Marco Baroni,Iuri Macocco*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了词汇多样性（数据集中唯一末词项的数量）如何影响语言模型表示的内在维度（ID）估计。发现存在一个与尺度相关的两阶段转变：低词汇多样性时，唯一词更少的条件产生更高ID；高词汇多样性时则反转。作者推导出一个无参数精确公式预测反转点，并在所有测试尺度上匹配观测结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内在维度被广泛用于探测语言模型的表示复杂度，但ID差异究竟反映语言本身的属性还是数据集构建方式的人为产物尚不明确。本文旨在厘清这一混淆因素，具体聚焦于词汇多样性对ID估计的影响。

Method: 作者系统地变化数据集的词汇多样性（即唯一末词项数量），在不同尺度上测量ID估计值，观察其变化规律。发现低/高词汇多样性区间存在ID排序反转，并推导出一个精确的、无参数的公式来计算反转发生的临界点，随后在多个尺度上对该公式进行验证。

Result: 发现ID与词汇多样性之间存在尺度依赖的非单调关系：低词汇多样性区间，唯一词少的条件ID更高；高词汇多样性区间则反转。推导的反转点公式在所有测试尺度上均与观测一致，精确预测了转变发生的位置。

Conclusion: 一方面提醒研究者解读ID时须谨慎，不能简单将其视为表示复杂度的直接指标；另一方面，两个ID阶段的发现揭示了LLM中语言数据组织的一般原则，为理解其内部流形结构提供了新视角。

Abstract: Intrinsic dimensionality (ID) is widely used to probe the representational complexity of language models, but it remains unclear whether ID differences reflect properties of language itself or artefacts of how the underlying dataset was constructed. In this paper, we focus specifically on how lexical diversity, the number of unique last-token items present in a dataset, affects ID estimates of that dataset. We find a scale-dependent transition between two regimes: at low lexical diversity, conditions with fewer unique final words produce higher ID, while at high lexical diversity, this ordering reverses, and conditions with more unique words produce higher ID. We derive an exact, parameter-free formula for the point at which this reversal occurs, which matches the observed transition point at every scale tested. On the one hand, our results highlight how care must be taken when interpreting the intrinsic dimensionality of a set of representations as a straightforward cue of their complexity. On the other hand, our discovery of the two ID regimes reveals a general principle of organisation of linguistic data in LLMs that sheds new light on their inner manifold structures.

</details>


### [97] [A Survey of Large Models in Sports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14377)
*Yichen Xu,Jianzhe Ma,Chuhan Wang,Zhonghao Cao,Liangyu Chen,Wenxuan Wang,Qin Jin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文全面综述了大型模型（特别是多模态大语言模型）在体育领域中的应用，涵盖任务与应用的分类、体育相关数据集与基准的详细分析，以及当前挑战与未来方向的批判性讨论，旨在为大型模型驱动的体育智能研究与实践奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着体育在全球范围内的热度持续增长，以及大型模型（尤其是多模态大语言模型）在理解、分析和交互方面展现出变革性潜力，作者认为有必要对该领域进行系统性的梳理与综述，以整合分散的研究成果并为后续研究提供参考框架。

Method: 作者采用综述研究的方法，从三个维度展开：(i)按不同参与者群体概述体育领域的任务与应用；(ii)详细分析体育相关数据集与基准；(iii)批判性地讨论当前面临的挑战与未来研究方向，并维护一个开源GitHub资源库作为配套材料。

Result: 论文通过系统的文献梳理，构建了大型模型在体育领域的任务-应用全景图，汇总了相关数据集与基准资源，并识别出该领域当前存在的关键挑战（如数据质量、多模态融合、领域适配等），为后续研究指明了方向。

Conclusion: 该综述为大型模型驱动的体育智能研究提供了基础性支撑，明确了该交叉领域的研究版图与未来机遇，并将通过持续维护的开源仓库推动社区协作与知识共享。

Abstract: Sports have witnessed growing global enthusiasm in recent years, serving as a vital force for physical health, cultural exchange, social connection, and economic growth. The rapid advancement of large models, particularly (multimodal) large language models (M)LLMs, has demonstrated transformative potential to reshape sports understanding, analysis, and interaction across diverse domains. This paper presents a comprehensive survey of large models in sports, including (i) an overview of tasks and applications across different participant groups; (ii) a detailed analysis of sports-related datasets and benchmarks; and (iii) a critical discussion of current challenges and future directions. Our goal is to establish a foundation for advancing research and practical development of large-model-driven sports intelligence. An open-source GitHub repository is maintained at: https://github.com/Road2Redemption/Awesome_Large_Models_In_Sports1.

</details>


### [98] [Information Satisfaction: A Reader-Centered Axis for Summarization Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14457)
*Isabel Cachola,William Walden,Reno Kriz,Mark Dredze*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨了摘要评估指标对用户个体信息需求（尤其是个性化背景/人物角色）的敏感性。研究发现，无论是传统指标（ROUGE、BERTScore）还是基于LLM的评估器，都难以通过信息内容的扰动测试，且与人类专家基于信息满意度的判断一致性很差。论文提出用读者背景（角色、专业）作为相对稳定的信号来评估摘要是否满足特定读者的信息需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有摘要评估工作主要关注通用质量（如ROUGE、BERTScore）或特定属性（如可读性、事实性），但无法衡量摘要对个体用户的实际效用。查询聚焦的摘要虽能部分捕捉需求，但用户很少在查询中完整表达需求；而读者的背景/人物角色相对稳定，可作为更好判断摘要信息满足度的信号。

Method: 1) 设计扰动测试评估主流摘要指标对信息内容差异和人物角色差异的敏感性；2) 开展专家人工评估，基于给定人物背景和使用场景衡量摘要的信息满意度；3) 对比传统指标与LLM-as-judge指标与人工判断的一致性。

Result: 许多主流指标（包括强LLM裁判指标）无法通过基础的信息内容扰动测试；传统指标和基于LLM的指标都不足以衡量信息满意度，与人类专家判断的一致性很差。

Conclusion: 现有摘要评估指标在衡量摘要对特定用户的信息效度方面存在根本性不足，需要开发能够捕捉用户个性化信息需求的新评估方法，而读者背景/人物角色是一个有前景的信号来源。

Abstract: The majority of work on summarization evaluation focuses on general summary quality (e.g., ROUGE, BERTScore) or specific desired properties (e.g., readability, factuality). However, these metrics fail to measure the utility of a summary to an individual user. For example, a biomedical researcher learning about the latest vaccine research will have different informational needs from a family doctor. Query-focused summarization captures part of this need, but in practice, users rarely state everything relevant in a query: a single short query is likely inadequate to distinguish the needs of a researcher from those of a physician. By contrast, a reader's background or persona (their role and expertise) is comparatively stable across queries and recovers much of this missing context, which makes it a practical signal for assessing whether a summary satisfies that reader's needs. In this work, we assess how sensitive popular summarization metrics are to both informational and persona differences, and find that many popular metrics, including strong LLM-as-judge metrics, fail basic perturbation tests of informational content. We additionally conduct an expert human evaluation, measuring summary preferences based on information satisfaction given a specific person's background and use case. We find that both traditional and LLM-based metrics are insufficient measures of information satisfaction and agree poorly with human judgment.

</details>


### [99] [You Only Pass Once: Answering and Abstaining Together in a Single Forward Pass of a Frozen Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14465)
*Ziyang Luo,Zhongyao Chu,Xinjie He,Youting Wang,Xukui Qin,Runxiong Wu,Yan-Syuan Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出YOPO系统，在冻结的Qwen2.5骨干网络上通过单次前向传播同时完成回答、推理引导（steering）和感知不足时弃权（abstain）。通过一个小型网络重构被steering后的残差流状态，避免了steering写入与sufficiency读取之间的干扰，在多个基准上超越双次前向传播的参照方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结语言模型在推理任务上存在两个耦合缺陷：一是未能充分利用自身残差流中已编码的证据信息；二是无法检测输入信息不足而进行臆造（confabulation）。此前有两个独立研究方向分别解决这两个问题（条件steering探针写入残差流提升推理准确率；零样本sufficiency方向读取残差流进行弃权），但当二者同时部署在单次前向传播中时会产生干扰。

Method: 保持sufficiency读取方向固定，训练一个小型网络从steering后的残差中重构出steering前的残差状态，使用(steered, clean)配对数据的均方误差进行训练，无需sufficiency标签。然后在重构后的残差上读取sufficiency方向来做弃权决策。整个系统YOPO（You Only Pass Once）对冻结的Qwen2.5骨干网络（1.5B/3B/7B）实现单次前向传播的问答、steering和弃权一体化。

Result: 端到端的三路准确率相比冻结基线提升一倍以上（1.5B alphaNLI上0.375→0.798）；单次前向传播在所有规模上均胜过双次前向参照方法（0.798/0.830/0.893 vs 0.753/0.790/0.863），并在六个模型家族的十个骨干网络上验证。作者还刻画了容量转移边界（capacity-transfer frontier），并通过源端审计发现了alphaNLI构造中的表面伪影泄漏，因此以原生标签数据集（SQuAD2、RepLiQA、MuSiQue）的结果为准。

Conclusion: 该研究表明弃权能力不应通过训练注入（abstention should not be trained in），label-free的sufficiency方向是唯一能在领域迁移后存活的gate家族。YOPO在标准四域套件上贡献了首个answer-or-abstain基准，其gate在所有域内数据集上达到最优。

Abstract: A frozen language model on reasoning tasks has two coupled weaknesses: it under-uses evidence its own residual stream already encodes, and it fails to detect when the input is insufficient to answer, so it confabulates. This paper consolidates two research lines that address these on the same residual stream: a conditional steering probe writes the stream at mid-stack layers and recovers reasoning accuracy from a frozen backbone, and a zero-shot sufficiency direction reads the stream and abstains when information is insufficient. Deployed in one forward pass they interfere: the steering write shifts the state the direction reads, costing up to 8 AUROC points of cross-domain transfer on small models; a separate clean pass doubles inference cost. We keep the direction fixed and train a small network to reconstruct the pre-steering residual from the steered one -- mean-squared error on (steered, clean) pairs, no sufficiency labels -- and read the direction on the reconstruction. The resulting system, YOPO (You Only Pass Once), answers, steers, and abstains in one forward pass of a frozen Qwen2.5 backbone (1.5B/3B/7B). End to end, three-way accuracy more than doubles the frozen baseline (0.375->0.798 on 1.5B alphaNLI) and one pass beats the two-pass reference at every scale (0.798/0.830/0.893 vs 0.753/0.790/0.863) and on ten backbones across six model families. We chart the capacity-transfer frontier quantifying the principle that abstention should not be trained in; a source-side audit catches our own alphaNLI construction leaking a surface artifact, so architectural claims are anchored on native-label replications (SQuAD2, RepLiQA, MuSiQue); and on the standard four-domain suite we contribute, to our knowledge, the first answer-or-abstain benchmark, where our gate tops every in-domain dataset and the label-free direction is the only gate family to survive domain transfer.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [100] [Inducing Reward-Free Judging Rubrics that Reduce Over-Crediting in Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13564)
*Darragh Quinn,David Dylan,Roisin Healy,Fionn Carroll,Maeve Donnelly,Cormac Sheehan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RubricForge，一种通过反射进化（reflective evolution）从少量带真实标签的轨迹中诱导出agent评判文本rubric的方法，冻结该rubric后可在单次模型调用中无需环境访问地评估held-out轨迹。实验表明其核心收益是忠实度（false-pass率更低）而非原始协议度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模评估语言模型agent日益依赖另一个语言模型作为自动裁判（LLM-as-judge），因为可执行环境奖励信号昂贵、缓慢或在部署时不可用。但现有裁判要么手写评分rubric（如G-Eval），要么微调裁判权重，这两者都倾向于把流畅但不成功的轨迹误判为成功。需要一种能依据真实结果、可被信任的奖励无关评估器。

Method: RubricForge通过反射进化（reflective evolution）演化出一个agent评判rubric，其优化目标是与环境奖励的一致性最大化。具体流程：从少量带ground-truth标签的轨迹中演化rubric→冻结该rubric→在单次模型调用中、无环境访问的情况下应用于held-out轨迹。优化产物为人类可读文本，因此每个判决都可归因到具名标准。

Result: 使用同一冻结的7B模型同时作为agent和judge，在tau-bench（220次rollout中抽取173条带标签轨迹）和WebShop（160条）上实验。主要收益是忠实度而非原始协议度：相对通用G-Eval裁判，协议度差异无统计学显著性（McNemar p=0.248），绝对分数校准甚至略逊于通用裁判（|err|差-0.048, p=2×10⁻⁴）。但RubricForge将失败轨迹误判为成功的概率约降低一半（tau-bench上false-pass率0.115 vs 0.173，三次过信用捕获、零反转），并在WebShop分级结果上更忠实（Spearman 0.410 vs 0.370）。

Conclusion: 对于无需环境奖励的评估器，false-pass率（而非聚合协议度）才是部署相关指标：false pass会放行一个有缺陷的agent上线，而false fail仅浪费一次重试。RubricForge在牺牲轻微协议度/校准精度的代价下大幅降低了false-pass率，使其更适用于高风险部署场景。

Abstract: Evaluating language-model agents at scale increasingly relies on a second language model as an automatic judge, because the gold signal, an executable environment reward, is expensive, slow, or unavailable at deployment time. Such a judge is a reward-free proxy whose value depends on whether it can be trusted, yet existing judges either hand-write the scoring rubric, as in G-Eval, or fine-tune the judge's weights, and both tend to credit fluent but unsuccessful trajectories as successes. We instead induce the text of an agent-judging rubric from a small set of ground-truth-labeled trajectories, grounding it in true outcomes. We present RubricForge, which evolves a judge rubric by reflective evolution against labeled trajectories to maximize agreement with the environment reward, freezes it, and applies it to held-out trajectories in one model call with no environment access. The optimized artifact is human-readable text, so every verdict is attributable to named criteria. Using one frozen 7B model as both agent and judge, on tau-bench (173 labeled trajectories drawn from 220 rollouts) and WebShop (160), the principal gain is faithfulness rather than raw agreement. The edge over a generic G-Eval judge is not statistically significant (McNemar p = 0.248), and absolute-score calibration marginally favors the generic judge (|err| difference -0.048, p = 2x10^-4). Yet RubricForge over-credits failed trajectories roughly half as often (0.115 vs. 0.173 false-pass rate on tau-bench, with three over-credit catches and zero reversals) and ranks graded WebShop outcomes more faithfully (Spearman 0.410 vs. 0.370). For a reward-free evaluator the false-pass rate, not aggregate agreement, is the deployment-relevant quantity, since a false pass ships a broken agent whereas a false fail merely costs a retry.

</details>


### [101] [Depth-Aware Sensitivity Analysis of Mixture-of-Experts Models via Magnitude-Based Expert Masking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13565)
*Pradeep Kumar Sharma,Shantanu Godbole,Hritvik Shrivastava*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对Qwen3.6-35B-A3B模型（40个MoE层、每层256个专家、top-8路由）进行了系统性的层间敏感性分析，发现层敏感性呈强深度依赖性：早期（0-9）和中期（10-29）层对专家掩蔽高度脆弱，而晚期层（30-39）尤其是极晚层（35-39）可容忍激进的低幅值专家掩蔽。在XLCoST跨语言代码翻译基准上，极晚层@50%掩蔽策略在500提示的保留验证集上实现了419/500的Good+Similar输出率，仅掩蔽10,240个总专家中的640个，是质量/掩蔽专家数权衡最优的策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合专家（MoE）架构通过稀疏激活在保持计算效率的同时扩展了大语言模型规模，但单个MoE层的相对重要性尚未得到充分表征，尤其是在模型压缩场景中。当前缺乏对MoE层间敏感性差异的系统性理解，这限制了深度感知的MoE压缩策略的设计。

Method: 基于幅值的专家掩蔽（magnitude-based expert masking）方法，在XLCoST跨语言代码翻译基准上对Qwen3.6-35B-A3B进行多阶段层间敏感性分析。研究涵盖100、300和500个提示的评估规模，在三台H100 GPU服务器上进行，比较了全层平坦掩蔽与各类晚期层聚焦策略的效果，并测试了top-k路由宽度从8降至6的影响。

Result: 全层30%掩蔽在300提示规模下仅保留150/300的Good+Similar输出，而晚期层聚焦策略在掩蔽640-1,145个专家的情况下保留249-255/300。在500提示的保留验证集上，极晚层（35-39层@50%）策略以掩蔽仅640/10,240个专家的代价保留419/500的Good+Similar输出。top-k从8降至6在100提示探针上显示了显著的墙钟时间减少且无Good+Similar损失，但与激进的专家掩蔽尚不能干净地组合。

Conclusion: 层敏感性呈强深度依赖性，晚期层对专家掩蔽更具鲁棒性，这为深度感知的MoE专家掩蔽提供了实证基础，并为物理权重手术、基于激活的专家评分以及训练恢复等后续工作指明了可行路径。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures scale large language models (LLMs) while preserving computational efficiency through sparse activation. Despite their widespread adoption, the relative importance of individual MoE layers remains insufficiently characterized, particularly for model compression. This paper presents a systematic layer-wise sensitivity analysis of the Qwen3.6-35B-A3B model (40 MoE layers, 256 experts per layer, top-8 routing) using magnitude-based expert masking on the XLCoST cross-lingual code translation benchmark. We conduct a multi-phase study spanning 100, 300, and 500 prompt evaluation scales across three H100 GPU servers. Our central finding is that layer sensitivity is strongly depth-dependent: early layers (0-9) and middle layers (10-29) are highly fragile to expert masking, while late layers (30-39), and especially very-late layers (35-39), tolerate aggressive masking of low-magnitude experts. Flat all-layer masking at 30% retains only 150/300 Good+Similar outputs at 300-prompt scale, whereas late-focused policies retain 249-255/300 while masking 640-1,145 experts. On a later 500-prompt held-out validation slice, the narrow very-late policy (layers 35-39 @ 50%) achieves the strongest quality/masked-expert tradeoff among tested candidates, retaining 419/500 Good+Similar outputs while masking only 640 of 10,240 total experts. We additionally characterize top-k routing width reduction from 8 to 6 active experts per token, which shows a large observed wall-clock reduction on a 100-prompt probe with no Good+Similar loss, though it does not yet compose cleanly with aggressive expert masking. These findings provide an empirical foundation for depth-aware MoE expert masking and establish a practical path toward physical weight surgery, activation-based expert scoring, and training-based recovery.

</details>


### [102] [Modular Cognitive Architecture Emerges in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13567)
*Pengrui Han,Jacob Andreas,Evelina Fedorenko,Andrea Gregor de Varda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过电路分析发现，大型语言模型（LLMs）在架构上出现了与人类大脑相似的模块化组织：来自同一认知领域（语言、形式推理、社会推理、物理推理）的任务会激活重叠的神经元，而不同领域的任务则激活不同的神经元，表明模块化可能是智能系统的普遍属性而非生物进化的偶然事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类大脑呈现高度功能特化（领域特异性网络），作者希望探究这种模块化组织是智能系统构建的普遍原理，还是仅存在于生物大脑的进化偶然事件。通过研究另一种通过截然不同优化过程产生的智能系统——大型语言模型——来检验这一问题。

Method: 研究使用电路分析（circuit analyses），在N=46个跨四个认知领域（语言、形式推理、社会推理、物理推理）的任务上，考察LLMs内部神经元的激活模式，并将其与人类大脑中对应该任务所涉及的功能网络进行比较。

Result: 研究发现LLMs发展出了与人类大脑镜像对应的模块化架构：在人类中涉及同一网络的任务在LLMs中会招募重叠的神经元，而涉及不同网络的任务则招募不同的神经元。

Conclusion: 大脑与神经网络中模块化的趋同出现表明，模块化可能是智能系统的基本属性，而非生物进化的特异性结果。

Abstract: The human brain exhibits a striking degree of functional specialization, with distinct networks supporting language, formal reasoning, reasoning about other minds, and reasoning about the physical world. Is this modular organization a fundamental principle of how intelligent systems must be built, or an evolutionary accident specific to biological brains? Here, we test whether a similar organization emerges in Large Language Models--another class of intelligent systems created through a very different optimization process. Using circuit analyses across N=46 tasks spanning four cognitive domains (language, formal reasoning, social reasoning, physical reasoning), we find that LLMs develop a modular architecture that mirrors the human brain: tasks drawing on the same network in humans recruit overlapping neurons in LLMs, whereas tasks drawing on different networks recruit distinct neurons. The convergent emergence of modularity in brains and neural networks suggests that it may be a fundamental property of intelligent systems.

</details>


### [103] [A Year in LLM Serving: Workload Evolution, Caching and Load-Balancing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13573)
*William Nixon,Jon Durbin,Florian Standhartinger,Haryadi S. Gunawi,Juncheng Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过对Chutes平台一年生产环境的完整追踪数据，进行了全球范围的特征描述和纵向研究，揭示了LLM服务工作负载的演化规律和用户-模型交互结构，并将发布完整一年的追踪数据以供后续研究使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM服务工作负载研究在规模和时间范围上有限，多观察短时间段，缺乏对用户与模型在生产中交互方式的深入可见性，无法充分捕捉工作负载随时间的演化以及用户-模型交互如何塑造生产流量。需要更全面、更长期的真实生产数据来有效激励和基准测试LLM服务系统。

Method: 从Chutes平台获取一年期的生产追踪数据，该数据捕获了跨多个模型和用户的完整生产行为，包括热门模型和长尾模型。通过聚合、时间、模型层级和用户层级四个视角分析工作负载，揭示被聚合视图掩盖的工作负载演化和用户-模型结构。

Result: 揭示了LLM服务工作负载随时间的演化规律以及用户-模型之间的交互结构，这些模式在传统的聚合视图中通常不可见。将从聚合、时间、模型和用户四个不同维度全面描述真实世界的工作负载特征。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了对真实世界LLM服务工作负载更深入的理解，并将发布完整的一年期追踪数据，支持下游研究者在不依赖采样或合成工作负载的情况下开展生产环境行为研究，推动LLM服务系统的进一步发展。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) serving has become a critical cloud workload, and realistic traces are essential for motivating and benchmarking serving systems. However, existing LLM serving workload studies remain limited in scale and scope. They often observe short time periods and provide limited visibility into how users interact with models in production. As a result, they do not fully capture how LLM serving workloads evolve over time or how user-model interactions shape production traffic.
  In this work, we further the understanding of real-world LLM serving workloads through both a global characterization and a longitudinal study of a one-year production trace from Chutes. Unlike prior studies, our trace captures full production behavior across many models and users, including both popular and long-tail models. We analyze the workload from aggregate, temporal, model-level, and user-level perspectives, revealing workload evolution and user-model structure that are typically hidden behind aggregate views. To support future research, we will release the full one-year trace with the paper, enabling downstream studies of production behavior without relying on sampled or synthetically generated workloads.

</details>


### [104] [Agentao: A Governed Local-First Runtime for Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13574)
*Bo Jin,Qiang Jiao,Xin Tong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Agentao是一个面向工具调用型LLM智能体的"本地优先+受治理"运行时系统，通过将模型生成的动作提案与主机授权的执行相分离，采用分层架构（主机接口层、主机契约、运行时核心、权限中介工具系统及内存、回放、插件等子系统），将权限、状态、协议边界和执行轨迹显式建模为运行时抽象，以提高智能体的可治理性、可检查性和对主机控制本地环境的适应性。系统不提供正式的安全保证，代码已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体作为执行系统的能力日益增强（工具调用、修改本地状态、持久内存、外部协议交互），但这些能力引入了过度授权操作、弱可审计性、提示注入、工具投毒及失控副作用等风险。现有系统缺乏将这些风险显式建模和治理的运行时抽象，因此需要一种能将权限和状态管理显式化的运行时架构。

Method: Agentao采用分层架构设计：主机面向接口层、主机契约、运行时核心、权限中介工具系统，以及支持子系统（内存、回放、插件、技能、子智能体、协议集成）。核心设计理念是将模型生成的动作提案与主机授权的执行相分离，通过权限机制、状态管理、协议边界和结构化事件接口，使治理逻辑成为运行时的一等公民。

Result: 论文描述了Agentao的动机、威胁模型、设计目标、治理模型、执行管线及结构化事件接口。系统成功地将权限、状态、协议边界和执行轨迹转化为显式的运行时抽象，但作者明确指出Agentao不提供正式的安全保证，其价值在于证明这些治理概念可以在运行时层面被具体化。

Conclusion: Agentao展示了构建更可治理、可检查且适合主机控制的本地环境的LLM智能体运行时的可行路径。通过将治理机制作为运行时显式抽象而非隐式假设，Agentao为后续在安全保证方向上进一步研究和强化提供了基础框架。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly operate as execution systems that invoke tools, modify local state, use persistent memory, and interact with external protocols. These capabilities make agents useful, but they also introduce risks related to over-privileged actions, weak auditability, prompt injection, tool poisoning, and uncontrolled side effects. This paper presents Agentao, a governed local-first runtime for tool-using LLM agents. Agentao separates model-generated action proposals from host-authorized execution through a layered architecture consisting of host-facing surfaces, a host contract, a runtime core, a permission-mediated tool system, and supporting subsystems for memory, replay, plugins, skills, sub-agents, and protocol integration. We describe the motivation, threat model, design goals, governance model, execution pipeline, and structured event interface of the system. Agentao does not provide formal safety guarantees; rather, it demonstrates how permissions, state, protocol boundaries, and execution traces can be made explicit runtime abstractions for building agents that are more governable, inspectable, and suitable for host-controlled local environments. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/jin-bo/agentao.

</details>


### [105] [AI Evaluation Should Work With Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13577)
*Jan Kulveit,Gavin Leech,Tomáš Gavenčiak,Raymond Douglas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出观点性论述，认为当前以超人类自主表现为主导的AI评估范式误导了AI发展方向，主张应转向评估人机协作团队的性能，以促进AI真正成为人类能力的补充，从而带来更好的社会结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者认为现有的AI评估范式隐含地以"替代人类"为目标（追求超人类自主表现），这一方向正在将AI发展引向错误路径，亟需纠正。

Method: 本文为观点论文（position paper），通过论证与逻辑推理提出评估范式转变的主张，即从评估单个AI的自主表现转向评估人机协作（human-AI team）的整体性能。

Result: 论文主张协作性转变将使AI系统真正成为人类能力的补充（complements），从而比当前评估流程带来更优的社会结果。

Conclusion: AI评估领域应从"替代人类"范式转向"人机协作"范式，这一转变将引导AI朝更有利于社会发展的方向前进。

Abstract: This position paper argues that the dominant paradigm of AI evaluation (which focuses on superhuman autonomous performance and so implicitly targets the goal of replacing humans) is guiding AI development in the wrong direction. Instead, the AI community should pivot to evaluating the performance of human--AI teams. We argue that this collaborative shift will foster AI systems that act as true complements to human capabilities and therefore lead to far better societal outcomes than will the current process.

</details>


### [106] [Stable Miscalibration in Large Language Models: A Practical View of High-Confidence Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13591)
*Akira Okutomi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型中高置信度错误的一种新解释——稳定误校准（stable miscalibration），而非仅视为内部推理脆弱性的证据。作者结合输出层面的标签感知审计分数与内部敏感性探针两种诊断方法，在多领域二元事实审计集上验证，并发现自批判提示能一致降低三层开源模型各层的隐藏状态敏感性，支持提示诱导的局部稳定化，但高置信错误并不比高置信正确答案更敏感，表明部分高置信错误可能是稳定且误校准的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型中高置信度错误通常被视为内部推理脆弱的证据，但作者提出另一种可能性：某些置信度高的错误在局部小扰动下保持稳定，即存在稳定的误校准现象。这挑战了"自信错误必然源于脆弱推理"的传统观点，需要新的诊断方法加以区分。

Method: 组合两种诊断方法：(1) 标签感知的输出层面审计分数，在强制回答基线（forced-answer baseline）下按置信度变化和过度自信错误对领域进行排序；(2) 内部敏感性探针，测量隐藏状态在扰动下的移动程度。在多领域二元事实审计集上验证，并使用三个开源权重模型测试自批判提示对隐藏状态敏感性的影响。

Result: 审计分数能够追踪基于弃权机制的自批判在何处降低决策损失，但直接标注的基线对同一增益的排序更强。在内部层面，自批判提示一致降低三个开源模型各层级的隐藏状态敏感性，支持提示诱导的局部稳定化而非纯输出层面的弃权模式。然而，审计定义的过度自信错误并不比高置信正确回答更具备局部敏感性，表明部分高置信错误是稳定且误校准的。

Conclusion: 高置信度错误不一定是推理脆弱的信号，可能存在一类稳定且误校准的错误。自批判提示引发的局部稳定化不仅在输出层面起作用，更体现在隐藏状态层面的敏感性降低。但这并不等同于校准改善——稳定误校准的存在意味着需要重新审视对高置信错误的理解和应对策略。

Abstract: High-confidence errors in large language models are often treated as evidence of fragile internal inference. We study a different possibility: stable miscalibration, where a confident wrong answer remains locally stable under small perturbations. We combine two diagnostics: a label-aware output-level audit score that ranks domains by confidence variation and overconfident mistakes under a forced-answer baseline, and an internal sensitivity probe that measures hidden-state movement. On a multi-domain binary factual audit set, this audit score tracks where abstention-aware self-critique reduces decision loss, although direct labeled baselines rank the same gain more strongly. Internally, self-critical prompting consistently reduces hidden-state sensitivity across layers in three open-weight models. This supports prompt-induced local stabilization rather than a purely output-level abstention pattern, but it does not imply calibration: audit-defined overconfident errors are not clearly more locally sensitive than confidently correct answers, so some high-confidence errors may be stable and miscalibrated rather than simply fragile.

</details>


### [107] [Measuring Cross-Task Behavioral Consistency in Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13598)
*Amritesh Banerjee,Pranil Raichura*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出行为一致性度量（BCM），用于量化agent执行轨迹的行为一致性。通过对约9000条轨迹的分析，发现跨任务一致性与任务内一致性可分离，且一致性不可简化为成功率，可作为结果指标的补充过程级可靠性信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有agent评估几乎完全依赖结果指标（如成功率），仅能捕捉agent是否成功，而无法反映其行为的一致性。以往研究只测量同任务可复现性，无法观察不同规模的一致性分离现象。作者论证行为一致性是一个独立、可度量的属性。

Method: BCM方法包含三个步骤：1）训练一个模型，从agent执行轨迹的行为特征预测任务成功；2）为每条轨迹导出特征归因向量；3）在agent系统内计算这些向量的平均两两相似度。实验覆盖了来自六个语言模型agent在软件工程任务上的约9000条轨迹。

Result: 核心发现包括：跨任务与任务内一致性是两个可分离的轴——某些系统在单任务上局部可复现但跨任务全局碎片化，另一些在两种尺度上都一致；一致性不可简化为成功率，成功率相当的系统在一致性上可能差异显著；前述（frontier）与开源模型间的一致性差距在控制任务难度后仍然存在。

Conclusion: BCM被定位为一种过程级可靠性信号，用于补充结果指标。作者明确说明了该指标有效成立的条件，强调其在评估agent行为一致性方面的独特价值。

Abstract: Agent evaluation relies almost entirely on outcome metrics such as success rate, which capture whether an agent succeeds but not how consistently it behaves. We argue that behavioral consistency across tasks is a distinct and measurable property, and we introduce the Behavioral Consistency Metric (BCM) to quantify it. BCM trains a model to predict task success from behavioral features of agent execution traces, derives a per-trajectory feature-attribution vector, and measures the mean pairwise similarity of these vectors within an agent system. Across roughly 9,000 trajectories from six language model agents on software engineering tasks, our central finding is that cross-task and within-task consistency are distinct axes that can diverge: some systems are locally reproducible, behaving similarly on repeated attempts at one task, yet globally fragmented, with no stable strategy across different tasks, while others are consistent at both scales. Prior work measures only same-task reproducibility and so cannot observe this separation. We further find that consistency is not reducible to success rate, since systems with comparable success can differ sharply in consistency, and that the frontier-versus-open-source consistency gap persists under a within-task control that holds task difficulty constant. We position BCM as a process-level reliability signal that complements outcome metrics, and we are explicit about the conditions under which it is meaningful.

</details>


### [108] [Cross-Disciplinary Taxonomy and Modeling of Misunderstanding Generation, Amplification, and Detection, from Pragmatics to AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13604)
*Babak Abbaschian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对AI中介通信时代中误解检测这一紧迫问题，将误解分析为分层过程（分歧产生→放大→检测/修复或持续），综合九个互不引用的研究领域，识别出十一个确切的失败模式，并将其映射到八个分析层级；提出一个正式模型，将信息与传播理论从信号传输扩展到意义重构，并提供源代码证据矩阵、编码手册和九个对话案例作为审计依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着通信从实时面对面互动转向AI中介渠道，交流者切断了传统修复机制所依赖的资源，而新的检测手段尚未及时建立，误解检测成为亟待解决的关键问题。现有研究零散分布在多个互不引用的领域，缺乏一个统一的、能定位机制发生点并按功能分类的误解分类框架。

Method: 作者综合九个通常互不引用的研究领域的文献，识别出十一个确切的失败模式；根据每个机制在传播过程中的具体操作点，归纳出八个分析层级（源自文献而非采用现成模型）；将这八个层级形式化建模，将信息与传播理论从信号传输扩展到意义重构；并构建证据矩阵使评级可审计，同时提供编码手册和九个对话案例。

Result: 识别出十一个失败模式中，八个主要生成分歧，两个主要放大已有分歧，一个决定分歧是否被检测和修复；八个分析层级被正式建模；提供了可审计的证据矩阵、编码手册和九个对话分析案例。该分类是首个既将机制定位于过程中具体点、又按功能对机制进行类型划分的误解分类框架。

Conclusion: 本文提出的分层误解模型及十一项失败模式分类，为AI中介通信时代的误解检测提供了理论基础和可操作的分析工具，填补了以往分类方法在定位与功能划分上的空白。

Abstract: Detection of misunderstanding is an urgent problem to solve because communication has moved away from real-time, in-person interaction and is increasingly handled by AI-mediated channels. This shift cuts communicators off from the resources repair depends on faster than new means of detection are being built. In this paper we analyse misunderstanding as a layered process in which a divergence is generated, may then be amplified, and is either detected and repaired or left to persist unnoticed. Consolidating accounts from nine fields of research that do not ordinarily cite one another, we identify eleven exact failure modes and show that each operates at a specific point in a communicative process rather than anywhere within it. Those points give eight analytical layers, derived from the literature rather than adopted from an existing model. Eight of the mechanisms primarily generate a divergence, two primarily amplify one already present, and one governs whether a divergence is detected and repaired. We model the eight layers formally, extending information and communication theory from the transmission of signals to the reconstruction of meaning, and we supply a source-by-source evidence matrix that makes every rating auditable, a coding manual, and nine analysed dialogue cases. No prior classification of misunderstanding both locates mechanisms at points in the process and types them by function.

</details>


### [109] [Active Perception for Embodied Disambiguation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13605)
*Yiwei Liu,Luwei Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向具身环境目标消歧的主动感知框架，以主动观察为信息获取核心，利用视觉语言模型根据累积视觉证据和交互信息决定继续观察、请求澄清还是完成目标选择，在真实机器人实验中统一了物理信息获取与用户意图澄清过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具身环境中，目标歧义不仅源自用户意图不明确，还可能由于当前观察中缺少任务相关的物理证据（如遮挡、视角受限、文字不可读、目标未被观察到）。现有交互式消歧方法主要依赖向用户提问获取信息，无法解决因观察不足导致的歧义问题。

Method: 提出主动感知框架，以主动观察作为信息获取的支柱，使用视觉语言模型基于累积视觉证据和交互信息进行决策，包括三种行为：继续观察、请求澄清或完成目标选择。主动观察既能直接恢复缺失的判别性证据，也能揭示对象名称、标签和语义属性，从而在仍需澄清时改进用户交互质量。

Result: 真实机器人实验表明，该框架能够将物理信息获取与用户意图澄清统一到一个具身消歧过程中，有效解决遮挡、受限视角等观察导致的歧义问题。

Conclusion: 主动观察应作为具身目标消歧的信息获取主干，与用户澄清相结合形成统一的消歧框架，能够更全面地处理由观察证据缺失和目标语义模糊共同引起的歧义。

Abstract: Natural language provides robots with a flexible task interface, but target ambiguity in embodied environments arises not only from user intent; it can also result from missing taskrelevant physical evidence in the current observation. Existing interactive disambiguation methods primarily obtain additional information by asking the user, whereas occlusion, restricted viewpoints, unreadable text, and unobserved targets require the robot to actively change its observation. We propose an active-perception framework for embodied target disambiguation that uses active observation as the backbone for information acquisition and uses a vision-language model to decide, on the basis of accumulated visual evidence and interaction information, whether to continue observing, request clarification, or complete target selection. Active observation can both directly recover missing discriminative evidence and reveal object names, labels, and semantic attributes, thereby improving user clarification when it remains necessary. Real-robot experiments show that the framework combines physical information acquisition and userintent clarification within a unified embodied disambiguation process.

</details>


### [110] [MobileMem: Learning from a Year of Mobile Experiences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13606)
*Xinle Deng,Yida Xue,Xiangyuan Ru,Haoming Xu,Shuofei Qiao,Mengru Wang,Yijun Chen,Buqiang Xu,Chen Jiang,Yuchen Eleanor Jiang,Lizhong Wang,Jianfeng Wang,Li Zeng,Haofen Wang,Guilin Qi,Huajun Chen,Ningyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MobileMem是一个面向设备端长期记忆的研究基准与框架，基于年度级移动体验数据，通过知识引导的合成管道构建连贯且时间一致的长跨度轨迹，支持文本和多模态设置，涵盖多跳推理、时间推理、知识更新和隐式偏好推断，推动AI助手从孤立问答走向经验性智能的持续个性化学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代AI助手正向具备长期记忆的持久个人助理演进，需要积累并利用用户特定经验。然而现有基准无法充分覆盖现实移动环境——这些环境中的经验具有异质性、多模态、不断演变和高度个人化等特点，因此亟需一个合适的长期记忆评估框架。

Method: MobileMem构建于年度级移动体验数据之上，采用知识引导的合成管道生成连贯且时间一致的长跨度用户-应用会话轨迹，并提供互补的文本与多模态设置，分别评估多跳与时间推理、知识更新以及隐式偏好推断等能力。

Result: MobileMem使智能体能够记住过去、理解当下并适应未来，通过建模经验而非孤立事实，将记忆从单纯的信息检索推进到经验性智能层面，从而实现持续的个性化学习。

Conclusion: MobileMem将长期记忆研究从信息检索范式转向经验性智能范式，为设备端持续个人学习提供了切实的基准框架，填补了现有评估体系在真实移动场景长期记忆能力上的空白。

Abstract: The next generation of AI agents is increasingly moving beyond systems that answer isolated questions toward persistent personal assistants that can understand, remember, and continuously learn from users' experiences. Such assistants require long-term memory to accumulate and leverage user-specific experiences over time, yet existing benchmarks remain inadequate for realistic mobile settings, where experiences are heterogeneous, multimodal, evolving, and deeply personal. We introduce MobileMem, a benchmark and framework for studying on-device long-term memory, grounded in a year-scale collection of mobile experiences. MobileMem employs a knowledge-grounded synthesis pipeline to construct coherent and temporally consistent long-horizon trajectories from user-app sessions. It provides complementary text and multimodal settings covering multi-hop and temporal reasoning, knowledge updating, and implicit preference inference. Specifically, MobileMem enables agents to remember the past, understand the present, and adapt to the future. By modeling experiences rather than isolated facts, MobileMem moves memory beyond information retrieval toward experiential intelligence for continuous personal learning.

</details>


### [111] [No Universal Signal Predicts Sample-Level LLM Regression under Version Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13607)
*Jia Sheng,Yiwei Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究如何在推理时利用可用信号预测大语言模型更新后出现的样本级回归（即旧模型回答正确但新模型回答错误的情况）。通过对六种信号（单模型信号与跨版本信号）在统一附加值测试框架下的比较，发现信号有效性高度依赖任务类型：置信度在单选题和简单数学上最强，似然/KL信号在难题和代码生成上更优；没有信号在所有模型更新下普遍最佳；部分跨版本信号在置信度失效时仍保持信息量，支撑了将高风险样本回退到旧模型的概念验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型频繁更新且总体性能提升，但总体增益无法反映个体样本层面的性能倒退（regression）。模型更新可能导致原本正确的回答在新版模型下变得错误，这种样本级回归需要被预测。而现有研究缺乏对不同预测信号的系统比较，尤其在统一的评测框架下评估各信号相对置信度基线的增量价值。

Method: 构建统一的"added-value"测试框架，将每个信号在置信度基线之上的增益进行隔离评估。比较六种信号：单模型信号（置信度、logit边际、注意力熵）与跨版本信号（输出KL散度、似然漂移、token级KL、表征漂移）。实验覆盖三个任务族（多选题问答、数学推理、代码生成）的六个数据集，以及六组模型更新对。

Result: 研究发现：（1）信号有效性具有任务依赖性——置信度在单选题和简单数学上最强，而似然/KL信号在较难数学和代码生成上带来最多增益；（2）没有任何信号在跨越所有模型更新时普遍最优；（3）某些跨版本信号即使在置信度失效的情况下仍能提供有用信息，且不依赖标签，从而支持了将高风险样本选择性回退到旧模型的概念验证方案。

Conclusion: 实践者可以根据任务层面的模式，为特定模型更新选择应信任的回归预测信号。该工作为模型版本更新时的性能监控和选择性回退机制提供了可操作的指导，并开源了代码以便复现和扩展。

Abstract: Frontier LLMs are updated frequently and typically outperform their predecessors in aggregate. But aggregate gains say little about individual samples: an update can still cause sample-level regression, where a response correct under the old model becomes incorrect under the new one. This paper studies how to predict such regressions from signals available at inference time. We compare single-model signals (confidence, logit margin, attention entropy) against cross-version signals (output KL divergence, likelihood drift, token-level KL, representation drift) under a unified added-value test that isolates each signal's gain over a confidence baseline. Across six benchmarks in three task families (multiple-choice question answering, or MCQ; math reasoning; code generation) and six model update pairs, we find that (1) signal effectiveness is task-dependent: confidence is strongest on MCQ and simpler math, while likelihood/KL signals give the most frequent gains on harder math and code; (2) no signal is universally best across model updates either; and (3) some cross-version signals stay informative even when confidence fails, including without labels, which supports a proof-of-concept selective fallback that routes high-risk samples back to the old model. Practitioners can use these task-level patterns to choose which regression signal to trust for a given update. Code is available at https://github.com/jiashengsally/llm-regression-signals.

</details>


### [112] [Evaluating Agentic Learning Harness Capabilities Without Labels via the Scaling Hypothesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13608)
*Aryan Luthra,Kshitij Jain,Siddharth Arya,Bobby Filar,Anna Bertiger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需标注基准测试即可评估智能体持续学习系统（LLM+检索/记忆）的方法，通过让更强的教师模型向带有持续学习能力的较弱学生模型提供稀疏修正，以学生向教师收敛的速度来评分该学习系统的效用，并在多个安全任务中验证了该指标与真实金标准提升的相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在网络安全操作场景中，标注基准测试稀缺、过时且不具代表性，导致从业者无法判断持续学习系统是否有效或哪个系统更优；传统的LLM-as-a-judge因与被评估的智能体能力相当而无法提供有效信号，知识蒸馏在稀疏、零星的偏差标签上也不可靠。

Method: 基于缩放假设（scaling hypothesis），采用更强教师模型对带持续学习系统的较弱学生模型提供稀疏采样修正，通过学生模型随时间向教师模型收敛的程度来对学习系统进行评分；将教师相对提升（teacher-relative lift）作为无标签场景下真实学习增益的代理指标，并在安全事故任务、不同模型家族和不同系统设计上验证该相关性。

Result: 在多种安全任务、模型家族和系统设计下，学生相对教师的提升与相对保留金标准的提升显著相关，验证了教师相对提升可作为无标签场景下真实系统增益的有效代理；同时发现同等算力模型之间的LLM-as-a-judge无法提供可用信号。

Conclusion: 教师相对提升可成为无标注环境下评估持续学习系统的可靠代理指标，并进一步表明当人类提供同类稀疏、高精度修正时，同规模教师模型可通过相同系统获得改进。

Abstract: Agentic "Continual Learning Harnesses", systems that pair an LLM with retrieval or memory to improve from feedback without retraining, have shown growing value in cybersecurity. But their value is conventionally measured by gains against labeled benchmarks, an approach that often fails in operational security settings. Benchmark labels are scarce, stale, and unrepresentative, so a practitioner often cannot tell whether a given harness helps at all or which of two is better for their task. Traditional LLM-as-a-judge offers little signal because it is no stronger than the agent it evaluates, and distillation is unreliable on scarce, sporadic, and biased labels.
  We propose a framework for evaluating learning harnesses end-to-end without a labeled benchmark, grounded in the scaling hypothesis. A stronger teacher model provides sparsely sampled corrections to a smaller student with a continual learning harness. We score a harness by how much its student converges toward the teacher over time. Across security tasks, model families, and harness designs, we show that improvement relative to the teacher correlates with improvement relative to a held-out gold standard, validating teacher-relative lift as a proxy for true harness uplift when labels are absent. We further show that LLM-as-a-judge between similarly powered models yields no usable signal. These results suggest that a teacher-sized model can be improved through the same harness when humans provide the same kind of sparse, high-precision corrections.

</details>


### [113] [SemPlan: Benchmarking Structured Semantic Planning for LLM-Based Queries over Enterprise Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13612)
*Bruno Santos Teixeira*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SemPlan Benchmark，一个包含1,800个英葡双语确定性合成用例的基准测试，用于评估自然语言企业数据接口的四种架构设计。结果表明A3（结构化语义请求+确定性规划）统计上优于其他架构，但绝对正确率总体偏低（25.67%），不同架构在正确性、策略合规、成本和稳定性之间呈现权衡关系，而非单一架构全面占优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业数据接口的自然语言层需要把模糊的用户请求转化为受管控、可执行的行为，同时控制非法查询、策略违背、成本和不确定性。现有工作缺乏对这一架构设计空间的系统性、确定性、双语评估，作者因此构建了基准来比较不同架构的权衡。

Method: 构建确定性合成双语基准（英、巴葡），含1,800个用例（1,200个冻结科学评测子集）。在同一模型配置下比较四种架构：A1直接SQL生成、A2有界工具代理、A3结构化语义请求生成+确定性规划执行、A4澄清/有状态语义规划变体。共4,800条主记录，另设150例稳定性子集测试可重复性。

Result: 绝对正确率较低：A1=22.25%、A2=22.58%、A3=25.67%、A4=24.25%。A3正确率最高且在预定配对分析中显著优于A1/A2/A4；A1的政策正确率最高且不安全/无效率最低；A4平均API成本最低且假拒绝率最低。稳定性子集正确可重复性为92.00%-98.67%。

Conclusion: 结果支持权衡式解释而非普适排名：增加结构性约束会改变失败模式和效率，但不会单调提升正确率，也无法根本解决歧义与多轮状态一致性问题。不同架构各有优劣，选择取决于对正确性、策略合规、成本等目标的权重。

Abstract: Natural-language interfaces to enterprise data must translate underspecified requests into governed, executable behavior while controlling invalid queries, policy failures, cost, and nondeterminism. SemPlan Benchmark evaluates this architectural design space with a deterministic synthetic bilingual benchmark containing 1,800 cases in English and Brazilian Portuguese; 1,200 cases form the frozen scientific evaluation subset. Four architectures are compared under the same model configuration: direct SQL generation (A1), a bounded tool-agent baseline (A2), structured semantic-request generation followed by deterministic planning and execution (A3), and a clarification/stateful semantic-plan variant (A4). Across 4,800 primary records, answer correctness was low in absolute terms: 22.25% for A1, 22.58% for A2, 25.67% for A3, and 24.25% for A4. A3 had the highest observed correctness and significantly exceeded A1, A2, and A4 in the pre-specified paired correctness analysis, while A1 retained the highest policy-correct rate and the lowest unsafe-or-invalid rate. A4 had the lowest mean API cost and lowest false-refusal rate. On a preselected 150-case stability subset, answer-correct repeatability ranged from 92.00% to 98.67%. The results support a trade-off interpretation rather than a universal ranking: additional structural constraints changed failure modes and efficiency, but did not monotonically improve correctness or solve ambiguity and multi-turn state consistency.

</details>


### [114] [How Compliant is Sepsis Treatment? An Expert-Guided Neuro-symbolic Pipeline for Generating Clinical Compliance Insights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13617)
*Himanshu Tripathi,Kaushik Roy,Subash Neupane,Shahram Rahimi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种专家引导的神经符号混合管线，用于评估脓毒症临床护理是否符合证据导向方案。大语言模型仅执行语义归一化任务，将凌乱的药物和微生物学字符串映射到固定临床词汇表，随后由Sugeno模糊推理系统对归一化事件进行推理，将八条脓毒症救治集束规则编码为[0,1]区间的分级评分。在2,438例MIMIC-IV v3.1脓毒症患者中应用发现抗生素时机是最关键的薄弱环节（均值0.24，仅13%在一小时内）、Hour-1表现不足（均值36.7%）、乳酸升高后51%的断崖式下降，以及不同依从组间ICU住院天数差异（3.8天对5.1天）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床护理是否遵循循证方案是一个天然的神经符号问题，但安全关键场景使得纯神经方法或纯符号方法单独使用均不可行，因此需要一种将两者结合且可验证的混合框架。

Method: 采用专家引导的双层管线：第一层用大语言模型执行严格受限的语义归一化，将非结构化药物与微生物字符串映射到固定临床词汇表；第二层用Sugeno模糊推理系统对归一化事件进行推理，编码八条脓毒症救治集束规则，以分级评分替代二元判定。

Result: 在2,438例MIMIC-IV v3.1脓毒症病例上，发现抗生素给药时机是最关键短板（平均分0.24，仅13%在一小时内完成）；Hour-1集束整体表现不足（均值36.7%）；高乳酸患者中51%出现后续监测断档；依从性较高组与较低组的ICU住院时长存在差异（3.8天vs 5.1天）。

Conclusion: 该方法证明大语言模型与模糊推理系统结合能够有效评估复杂临床流程中的方案依从性，并揭示出脓毒症救治中抗生素时机及乳酸监测等关键质量缺口。

Abstract: Verifying whether clinical care follows evidence-based protocols is a natural neuro-symbolic problem, yet the safety-critical setting defeats either paradigm alone. We present an expert-guided pipeline that constrains a large language model strictly to semantic normalization, mapping messy drug and microbiology strings onto a fixed clinical vocabulary, while a Sugeno fuzzy inference system reasons over the normalized events. The fuzzy layer encodes eight Surviving Sepsis Campaign bundle rules and replaces binary judgments with graded scores in [0,1]. Applied to 2,438 MIMIC-IV v3.1 sepsis episodes, it surfaces antibiotic timing as the most critical breakdown (mean 0.24, 13% within one hour), Hour-1 underperformance (mean 36.7%), a 51% elevated-lactate drop-off, and descriptive differences in ICU stay across compliance groups (3.8 versus 5.1 days).

</details>


### [115] [Algorithm Design and Physician Liability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13618)
*Shujie Luan,Shubhranshu Singh,Tinglong Dai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究美国医疗AI责任规则如何重塑AI公司的算法设计决策与医生的AI使用决策。责任规则可能引发AI使用的不均衡：在中等责任区间，医生对弱势群体的AI使用率下降；随着责任增加，AI公司重新配置投资减少差异，医生对弱势群体的AI使用先降后升。强制算法平等精度反而可能同时损害两个群体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI在临床决策中的普及，单一临床算法在不同患者群体间可能存在精度差异，引发公平性担忧。美国引入的责任规则规定，当医疗提供者依赖有偏差的算法导致错误临床决策时需承担责任。作者希望理解这种责任机制如何重塑AI公司的算法设计动机和医生的AI使用决策，特别是对弱势群体的影响。

Method: 构建一个双主体博弈模型：AI公司为两个患者群体设计算法，提升弱势群体精度成本更高；医生作为最终责任承担者，权衡临床不确定性降低与预期责任暴露来决定是否使用AI。分析不同责任水平下公司投资配置与医生AI使用决策的均衡，并考察强制等精度要求的政策效果。

Result: 责任规则可诱导差异化的AI使用：医生可能整体减少AI使用，且在中等责任区间对弱势群体使用更少。该效应非单调——随着责任增加，医生对弱势群体的AI使用先降后升（因公司重新投资减少差异或转向等精度设计）。强制等精度要求因扭曲公司投资激励和医生均衡使用决策，反而可能伤害两个群体。

Conclusion: 责任规则与算法公平之间存在复杂的非线性互动关系。设计不当的公平性强制政策可能适得其反，政策制定者需考虑责任规则对AI公司投资激励和医生采用决策的整体均衡影响，而非仅追求表面上的算法精度平等。

Abstract: A single clinical algorithm can deliver unequal accuracy across patient groups, and concern about such disparity has grown as artificial intelligence (AI) spreads through clinical decision-making. In response, a liability rule introduced in the United States holds healthcare providers responsible when their reliance on disparate algorithms contributes to erroneous clinical decisions. We examine how such liability considerations reshape (i) an AI firm's algorithm design decisions that drive group-specific accuracy and (ii) a physician's decisions to use AI in healthcare delivery. The AI firm designs an algorithm for two patient groups, and improving accuracy for the disadvantaged group is more costly. The physician (who remains the accountable decision-maker) then decides whether to consult AI, weighing the reduction in clinical uncertainty against expected liability exposure when AI errors disproportionately affect the disadvantaged group. We find the liability rule can induce disparate use of AI: the physician may reduce AI use overall and, over an intermediate range of liability, rely on AI less for disadvantaged patients. The effect is non-monotone. As liability increases, the physician's use of AI for disadvantaged patients first declines, then rises as the firm reallocates investment toward reducing disparity or switches to an equal-accuracy design. Mandating equal algorithmic accuracy across patient groups can then inadvertently harm both groups, because a uniform accuracy requirement distorts the firm's investment incentives and the physician's equilibrium AI-use decisions.

</details>


### [116] [Your Probabilistic JEPA Is Secretly a Hidden Markov Model: A State-Space Interpretation of Joint-Embedding Predictive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13621)
*Yongchao Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: A hidden Markov model (HMM) combines three roles: inference of a hidden-state belief from observations, propagation through a Markov transition, and emission back to observation space. We show that full, time-indexed Predictive Information Bottleneck VJEPA (PIB-VJEPA) exposes the same computational structure: a stochastic context encoder plays the role of an amortized filtering distribution, a probabilistic predictor defines latent-state dynamics, and a decoder, inverse target encoder, or induced implicit conditional supplies the emission direction. We distinguish 4 progressively stronger levels of correspondence and give sufficient conditions for exact sequence-level HMM equivalence. To make the connection concrete, we introduce Markov-Chain JEPA (MCJEPA), which replaces the latent predictor by a learned transition matrix; in the finite time-homogeneous case, matrix powers guarantee exact multi-horizon Chapman--Kolmogorov consistency. Conditioned discrete-state transitions, continuous-state Markov kernels, and continuous-time dynamics extend this construction, while deterministic temporal JEPA appears as a degenerate Dirac-kernel special case. We further interpret predictive information-bottleneck learning as seeking a compact predictive state: compression promotes minimality, while residual predictability tests sufficiency. Controlled experiments support transition composition, the filtering interpretation, predictive Markovization in a known synthetic process, and the distinction between JEPA latent prediction and HMM-style sequence learning. Together, these results give temporal JEPA a principled state-space interpretation.

</details>


### [117] [ARC: Fair Relative Advantage Comparison in Open-Ended Real-World Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13622)
*Yongqi Tong,Tan Li Hui Faith,Choy Zhen Wen Marcus,Zhou Jin,Kewei Fu,Jiang-Ming Yang,Jianshe Li,Xin Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ARC（基于条件化的优势正则化）训练方法，解决开放式交互中强化学习因行为多样导致的奖励不公平比较问题，并配套提出inter交互范式及inter-86K训练语料。ARC通过策略条件化分组、混合奖励和熵正则化恢复公平的相对比较，在工具使用基准上显著提升性能，同时inter将首token延迟从4.91s降至1.27s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放式真实世界交互允许多种合理行为（直接回答、澄清、进度更新、行动前确认等），这打破了组基强化学习（group-based RL）的核心假设：同一组内的rollout不再保证行为上的可比性。这种灵活性导致奖励模型对交互风格的偏好可能扭曲相对优势，使优化朝向奖励偏好的行为而非上下文恰当的行为，作者将其形式化为"奖励公平性问题"（reward fairness problem）。

Method: 提出ARC（Advantage Regularization via Conditioning）训练方案：(1) 通过策略条件化rollout分组（strategy-conditioned rollout grouping）恢复更公平的相对比较；(2) 结合混合奖励（hybrid rewards）和熵正则化（entropy regularization）。同时提出inter交互范式，将用户可见的通信与潜在推理和工具使用解耦，实现响应式、可操控、执行感知的用户-智能体交互，并提供inter-86K注释与蒸馏训练语料构建流程。

Result: ARC显著增强了核心τ/τ²工具使用基准的性能；inter将首token生成时间从4.91s降低到1.27s（对比think风格基线）。

Conclusion: 开放式交互学习中的核心瓶颈不仅在于智能体如何被奖励（how agents are rewarded），还在于其行为是否首先被公平比较（whether their behaviors are compared fairly）。ARC实现和inter-86K训练数据将公开发布。

Abstract: Open-ended real-world interaction admits multiple valid behaviors: an agent may answer directly, ask for clarification, provide progress updates, or confirm before acting. This flexibility breaks a core assumption behind group-based RL: rollouts compared within a group are no longer guaranteed to be behaviorally comparable. As a result, reward-model preferences over interaction style can distort relative advantages and steer optimization toward reward-preferred behaviors rather than context-appropriate ones. We formalize this as a \textit{reward fairness problem} and propose \textbf{ARC} (Advantage Regularization via Conditioning), a training recipe that restores fairer relative comparison through strategy-conditioned rollout grouping, together with hybrid rewards and entropy regularization. We study ARC in our proposed \inter, a novel paradigm for responsive, steerable, and execution-aware user-agent interaction that decouples user-visible communication from latent reasoning and tool use. \inter\ also provides the annotation and distillation pipeline for constructing \inter-86K, our strategy-annotated training corpus for supervised and RL training. Empirically, ARC substantially strengthens the core $τ/τ^2$ tool-use benchmarks, while \inter\ reduces time-to-first-token from 4.91s to 1.27s relative to a think-style baseline. Together, these results suggest that a central bottleneck in open-ended interactive learning is not only how agents are rewarded, but whether their behaviors are compared fairly in the first place. The ARC implementation and \inter-86K training data will be released.

</details>


### [118] [Reward Machines for Signal Temporal Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13625)
*Alper Kamil Bozkurt,Shangtong Zhang,Yuichi Motai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于自动机的STL（信号时序逻辑）控制合成方法，通过构造时间交替自动机来为强化学习提供高效的内存机制和马尔可夫奖励，解决了传统STL鲁棒性奖励因依赖执行历史而导致状态空间爆炸的问题，实验表明该方法在鲁棒性分数和满足率上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代自主和AI系统往往缺乏精确完整的系统模型，使得基于优化的合成方法不适用，因此需要基于学习的控制方法。已有的RL方法使用STL鲁棒性分数作为奖励，但鲁棒性依赖执行历史，对于包含任意嵌套时间算子的长时域规范会导致状态空间不可处理的膨胀，亟需一种高效的内存机制来解决此问题。

Method: 从给定的STL规范构造一个时间交替自动机，将自动机的位置和时钟估值扩充到状态空间中，从自动机的接受条件推导出马尔可夫奖励，从而使奖励仅依赖于当前状态而无需依赖完整执行历史，适合RL框架使用。

Result: 实验证明，该方法学到的策略相比使用鲁棒性奖励的现有方法，能够获得更高的鲁棒性分数和更高的规范满足率。

Conclusion: 基于自动机的内存机制和马尔可夫奖励为STL控制合成提供了一种更高效的RL解决方案，能够应对长时域且具有任意嵌套时间算子的复杂规范。

Abstract: Signal temporal logic (STL) provides a formal language for specifying real-time properties of real-valued observations, along with a quantitative robustness score for monitoring satisfaction. Control synthesis from STL specifications is of interest since manual controller design becomes infeasible as real-world systems grow in complexity.
